 -- NXT On Top | Mobile + PC (UNC / Synapse / Fluxus / Solara / Delta / Wave / etc.)
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer
-- Prevent double-inject (stacked Heartbeats caused random physics deaths / respawns)
if getgenv then
    if getgenv()._NXTOnTopRunning then
        return
    end
    getgenv()._NXTOnTopRunning = true
end

-- Load persisted intro preference from session (disk load happens later in loadConfigKeys)
pcall(function()
    if getgenv and type(getgenv()._NXTOnTopCfg)=="table" and getgenv()._NXTOnTopCfg.introEnabled ~= nil then
        -- will be applied again after disk load; session hint only
    end
end)

-- ============================
-- EXECUTOR COMPAT (PC + Mobile)
-- ============================
local function _resolve(name)
    local g = rawget(_G, name) or (getgenv and getgenv()[name])
    if typeof(g)=="function" then return g end
    if syn and typeof(syn[name])=="function" then return syn[name] end
    if fluxus and typeof(fluxus[name])=="function" then return fluxus[name] end
    return nil
end

local _gethui = _resolve("gethui") or (typeof(gethui)=="function" and gethui) or nil
local _getcustomasset = _resolve("getcustomasset") or (typeof(getcustomasset)=="function" and getcustomasset) or nil
local _request = _resolve("request")
    or _resolve("http_request")
    or (syn and syn.request)
    or (http and (http.request or http.Request))
    or (fluxus and fluxus.request)
    or (typeof(request)=="function" and request)
    or (typeof(http_request)=="function" and http_request)
    or nil

local function protectGui(gui)
    if not gui then return end
    pcall(function()
        if typeof(protect_gui)=="function" then protect_gui(gui)
        elseif syn and syn.protect_gui then syn.protect_gui(gui)
        elseif typeof(hide_in_gcoregui)=="function" then hide_in_gcoregui(gui)
        end
    end)
end

local function parentGui(gui)
    if not gui then return end
    protectGui(gui)
    local ok = false
    if _gethui then
        ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = _gethui() end)
    end
    if not ok or not gui.Parent then
        ok = pcall(function() gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    end
    if not gui.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
            if plr then gui.Parent = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
        end)
    end
end

-- File API polyfills (Synapse / Fluxus / Delta / UNC / Solara / Wave)
local _isfile = (typeof(isfile)=="function" and isfile)
    or (syn and syn.isfile)
    or (fluxus and fluxus.isfile)
    or (getgenv and getgenv().isfile)
    or function(p)
        local ok, data = pcall(function() return readfile(p) end)
        return ok and data ~= nil
    end
local _readfile = (typeof(readfile)=="function" and readfile)
    or (syn and syn.readfile)
    or (fluxus and fluxus.readfile)
    or (getgenv and getgenv().readfile)
    or function() return nil end
local _writefile = (typeof(writefile)=="function" and writefile)
    or (syn and syn.writefile)
    or (fluxus and fluxus.writefile)
    or (getgenv and getgenv().writefile)
    or function() end
isfile, readfile, writefile = _isfile, _readfile, _writefile

-- Unified HTTP get (binary-safe when request exists)
local function nxtHttpGet(url)
    if _request then
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            return _request({
                Url = url,
                Method = "GET",
                Headers = { ["User-Agent"] = "Mozilla/5.0", ["Referer"] = "https://www.free-stock-music.com/" },
            })
        end)
        if ok and type(res)=="table" then
            return res.Body or res.body or res.Data or res.data
        elseif ok and type(res)=="string" then
            return res
        end
    end
    local ok2, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
    if ok2 then return body end
    return nil
end


-- NXT LAGGER is loaded only from its main-menu launcher.
local NXT_LAGGER_URL = "https://files.manuscdn.com/user_upload_by_module/session_file/310519663943627270/YVjUCbaSoXsHfYRD.txt"
local nxtLaggerLoaded = false
local function runNxtLagger()
    if nxtLaggerLoaded then return true end
    local source = nxtHttpGet(NXT_LAGGER_URL)
    if type(source) ~= "string" or #source < 100 then return false end
    source = source:gsub("KillHubConfig%.json", "NxtLaggerConfig.json")
    source = source:gsub("KillHub_UI", "NxtLaggerUI")
    source = source:gsub("KILL HUB", "NXT LAGGER")
    local compiler = loadstring or load
    if type(compiler) ~= "function" then
        warn("[NXT] AUTO PLY compiler unavailable")
        return false
    end
    local ok, chunk = pcall(compiler, source)
    if not ok or type(chunk) ~= "function" then
        warn("[NXT] AUTO PLY compile failed: " .. tostring(chunk))
        return false
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local ran, err = pcall(chunk)
        if not ran then warn("[NXT] AUTO PLY runtime failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
    end)
    nxtLaggerLoaded = true
    return true
end

-- NXT ANTI TP BAT opens from the main menu on demand.
local NXT_ANTI_TP_BAT_URL = "https://files.manuscdn.com/user_upload_by_module/session_file/310519663943627270/ccoCswWnrXYEqNvp.txt"
local nxtAntiTpBatLoaded=false
local function runNxtAntiTpBat()
    if nxtAntiTpBatLoaded then return true end
    local source=nxtHttpGet(NXT_ANTI_TP_BAT_URL)
    if type(source)~="string" or #source<100 then return false end
    source=source:gsub("__VYNX_ANTI_ANTI_TP","__NXT_ANTI_TP_BAT")
    source=source:gsub("VynxAntiAntiTP","NXTAntiTPBatUI")
    source=source:gsub("VYNX ANTI ANTI TP","NXT ANTI TP BAT")
    local compiler=loadstring or load
    if type(compiler)~="function" then return false end
    local ok,chunk=pcall(compiler,source)
    if not ok or type(chunk)~="function" then return false end
    task.spawn(function() pcall(chunk) end)
    nxtAntiTpBatLoaded=true
    return true
end


-- PING LEEGR action supplied in Pasted_content_38.txt
local function runPingLeegr()
    local _pingOk, _pingErr = pcall(function()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
    local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
    local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
    local ContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
    local RenderSettings = settings().Rendering
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    if playerGui:FindFirstChild("SourcesHubGUI") then
        playerGui.SourcesHubGUI:Destroy()
    end

    local CONFIG = {
        MAX_POWER = 150000,
        MIN_POWER = 10000,
        STEP = 1000,
        MAX_DELAY = 0.5,
        MIN_DELAY = 0.001,
        DEPTH = 186,
        REMOTE_NAMES = {"SetPlayerBlockList", "UpdatePlayerBlockList", "SetBlockList", "UpdateBlockList"},
        DISCORD = "https://discord.gg/nxt1",
        SAVE_FILE = "SourcesHub_Config.json",
        SAVE_INTERVAL = 8
    }

    local State = {
        running = false,
        bypassEnabled = false,
        mode = "Mobile",
        power = { PC = 100000, Mobile = 72000 },
        spamDelay = 0.125,
        keybind = Enum.KeyCode.C,
        thread = nil,
        payload = nil,
        remote = nil,
        lastSaveHash = "",
        executorFixer = false,
        executorFixerThreads = {},
        activeVersion = "V1",
        v1Delay = 0.125,
    }

    local canFile = (writefile and readfile and isfile) and true or false

    local function getStateHash()
        return State.power.PC .. "_" .. State.power.Mobile .. "_" .. State.mode .. "_" .. State.keybind.Name
    end

    local function saveConfig()
        if not canFile then return end
        local hash = getStateHash()
        if hash == State.lastSaveHash then return end
        State.lastSaveHash = hash
        local data = HttpService:JSONEncode({
            powerPC = State.power.PC,
            powerMobile = State.power.Mobile,
            mode = State.mode,
            keybind = State.keybind.Name
        })
        task.defer(function() pcall(writefile, CONFIG.SAVE_FILE, data) end)
    end

    local function loadConfig()
        if not canFile then return false end
        local ok, result = pcall(function()
            if not isfile(CONFIG.SAVE_FILE) then return nil end
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(CONFIG.SAVE_FILE))
        end)
        if not ok or not result then return false end
        if result.powerPC then State.power.PC = math.clamp(result.powerPC, CONFIG.MIN_POWER, CONFIG.MAX_POWER) end
        if result.powerMobile then State.power.Mobile = math.clamp(result.powerMobile, CONFIG.MIN_POWER, 100000) end
        if result.mode and (result.mode == "PC" or result.mode == "Mobile") then State.mode = result.mode end
        if result.keybind then
            local s, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[result.keybind] end)
            if s and kc then State.keybind = kc end
        end
        State.lastSaveHash = getStateHash()
        return true
    end

    loadConfig()

    local autoSaveAlive = true
    task.defer(function()
        while autoSaveAlive do
            task.wait(CONFIG.SAVE_INTERVAL)
            saveConfig()
        end
    end)

    --// Executor Fixer
    local ExecutorFixer = {}
    local OriginalValues = {
        qualityLevel = nil,
        lightingEffects = {},
        globalShadows = nil,
        fogEnd = nil,
        meshLOD = nil,
    }

    local PingStats = {
        current = 0,
        avg = 0,
        samples = {},
        maxSamples = 30,
        lastTeleportBlock = 0,
        tpbCount = 0,
    }

    local function measurePing()
        local ok, v = pcall(function()
            return Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
        end)
        if ok and type(v) == "number" and v > 0 then return v end
        local t = tick()
        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        return (tick() - t) * 1000
    end

    local function updatePingStats()
        local p = measurePing()
        PingStats.current = p
        table.insert(PingStats.samples, p)
        if #PingStats.samples > PingStats.maxSamples then table.remove(PingStats.samples, 1) end
        local s = 0
        for _, v in ipairs(PingStats.samples) do s = s + v end
        PingStats.avg = s / #PingStats.samples
    end

    local function applyGraphicsNuke()
        OriginalValues.qualityLevel = RenderSettings.QualityLevel
        pcall(function() RenderSettings.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 end)
        pcall(function()
            for _, effect in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                if effect:IsA("PostEffect") or effect:IsA("Sky") then
                    OriginalValues.lightingEffects[effect] = effect.Enabled ~= nil and effect.Enabled or true
                    if effect.Enabled ~= nil then effect.Enabled = false end
                end
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            OriginalValues.globalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows
            OriginalValues.fogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
            Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
            Lighting.Brightness = 2
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain") then
                Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain").Decoration = false
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            OriginalValues.meshLOD = settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel
            settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = Enum.MeshPartDetailLevel.Disabled
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
                Workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView = 70
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            for _, v in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") or v:IsA("Smoke")
                or v:IsA("Fire") or v:IsA("Sparkles") then
                    v.Enabled = false
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function restoreGraphics()
        pcall(function()
            if OriginalValues.qualityLevel then
                RenderSettings.QualityLevel = OriginalValues.qualityLevel
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            for effect, wasEnabled in pairs(OriginalValues.lightingEffects) do
                if effect and effect.Parent then effect.Enabled = wasEnabled end
            end
            OriginalValues.lightingEffects = {}
        end)
        pcall(function()
            Lighting.GlobalShadows = OriginalValues.globalShadows ~= nil and OriginalValues.globalShadows or true
            Lighting.FogEnd = OriginalValues.fogEnd or 100000
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain") then
                Workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain").Decoration = true
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            if OriginalValues.meshLOD then
                settings().Rendering.MeshPartDetailLevel = OriginalValues.meshLOD
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            for _, v in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Trail") or v:IsA("Smoke")
                or v:IsA("Fire") or v:IsA("Sparkles") then
                    v.Enabled = true
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function startAntiTPBack()
        local char = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local lastGoodPos = hrp and hrp.Position or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        local lastGoodTime = tick()
        local TPB_THRESHOLD = 40
        local TPB_TIMEWINDOW = 0.2

        local t = task.spawn(function()
            while State.executorFixer do
                task.wait(0.05)
                if not char or not char.Parent then
                    char = player.Character
                    if not char then task.wait(1) continue end
                    hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not hrp then task.wait(0.5) continue end
                    lastGoodPos = hrp.Position
                    lastGoodTime = tick()
                end
                if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then
                    hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    continue
                end
                local currentPos = hrp.Position
                local dist = (currentPos - lastGoodPos).Magnitude
                local dt = tick() - lastGoodTime
                if dist > TPB_THRESHOLD and dt < TPB_TIMEWINDOW then
                    pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(lastGoodPos) end)
                    PingStats.lastTeleportBlock = tick()
                    PingStats.tpbCount = PingStats.tpbCount + 1
                else
                    if dt >= 0.3 then
                        lastGoodPos = currentPos
                        lastGoodTime = tick()
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        local respawnConn
        respawnConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            if not State.executorFixer then respawnConn:Disconnect() return end
            task.wait(1)
            char = newChar
            hrp = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if hrp then
                lastGoodPos = hrp.Position
                lastGoodTime = tick()
            end
        end)

        return t, respawnConn
    end

    local function startNetworkOptimizer()
        return task.spawn(function()
            local gcTick = 0
            while State.executorFixer do
                task.wait(0.25)
                gcTick = gcTick + 1
                updatePingStats()
                if State.running and State.activeVersion == "V2" then
                    if PingStats.avg > 300 then
                        State.spamDelay = math.min(State.spamDelay * 1.3, CONFIG.MAX_DELAY)
                    elseif PingStats.avg > 150 then
                        State.spamDelay = math.min(State.spamDelay * 1.05, CONFIG.MAX_DELAY)
                    elseif PingStats.avg < 60 then
                        State.spamDelay = math.max(State.spamDelay * 0.97, CONFIG.MIN_DELAY)
                    end
                end
                pcall(function()
                    if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
                        player.ReplicationFocus = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                    end
                end)
                if gcTick % 60 == 0 then
                    task.defer(function()
                        pcall(function() ContentProvider:PreloadAsync({}) end)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function startFPSStabilizer()
        return task.spawn(function()
            local lastT = tick()
            local gcFrame = 0
            while State.executorFixer do
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                gcFrame = gcFrame + 1
                if gcFrame >= 200 then
                    gcFrame = 0
                    task.defer(function()
                        local _ = {}
                        for i = 1, 1000 do _[i] = i end
                        _ = nil
                    end)
                end
                local now = tick()
                if now - lastT >= 2 then
                    lastT = now
                    pcall(function()
                        if RenderSettings.QualityLevel ~= Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 then
                            RenderSettings.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
                        end
                        Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function ExecutorFixer.start()
        if State.executorFixer then return end
        State.executorFixer = true
        applyGraphicsNuke()
        local t1 = startFPSStabilizer()
        local t2 = startNetworkOptimizer()
        local t3, conn3 = startAntiTPBack()
        State.executorFixerThreads = {t1, t2, t3, conn3}
    end

    function ExecutorFixer.stop()
        State.executorFixer = false
        for _, v in ipairs(State.executorFixerThreads) do
            if type(v) == "thread" then pcall(task.cancel, v)
            elseif type(v) == "table" and v.Disconnect then pcall(function() v:Disconnect() end) end
        end
        State.executorFixerThreads = {}
        restoreGraphics()
        pcall(function() player.ReplicationFocus = nil end)
    end

    --// Core bypasses
    local function findRemote()
        local ok, rrs = pcall(function()
            return game:GetService("RobloxReplicatedStorage")
        end)
        if not ok or not rrs then return nil end
        for _, name in ipairs(CONFIG.REMOTE_NAMES) do
            local r = rrs:FindFirstChild(name)
            if r and r:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return r end
        end
        for _, c in ipairs(rrs:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("RemoteEvent") and c.Name:find("Block") then return c end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function buildPayload(power)
        local main = {}
        local nested = {{}}
        local current = nested[1]
        for _ = 1, CONFIG.DEPTH do
            local n = {}
            table.insert(current, n)
            current = n
        end
        local maxRep = math.min(math.floor(power / (CONFIG.DEPTH + 2)), 10000)
        for _ = 1, maxRep do table.insert(main, nested) end
        return main
    end

    local function stopExecution()
        if State.thread then pcall(task.cancel, State.thread) State.thread = nil end
        State.payload = nil
    end

    local function startExecution()
        if not State.remote then State.remote = findRemote() end
        
        if State.activeVersion == "V1" then
            local power = State.power[State.mode] or 100000
            State.payload = buildPayload(power)
            State.thread = task.spawn(function()
                while State.running do
                    if State.remote and State.payload then
                        pcall(function() State.remote:FireServer(State.payload) end)
                    end
                    task.wait(State.v1Delay or 0.125)
                end
            end)
        else
            State.payload = buildPayload(State.power[State.mode])
            local delay = State.spamDelay
            State.thread = task.spawn(function()
                while State.running do
                    if State.remote and State.payload then
                        local ok = pcall(function() State.remote:FireServer(State.payload) end)
                        if not ok then delay = math.min(delay * 1.5, CONFIG.MAX_DELAY)
                        else delay = math.max(delay * 0.995, CONFIG.MIN_DELAY) end
                    end
                    task.wait(delay)
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    --// GUI Initialization
    local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    ScreenGui.Name = "SourcesHubGUI"
    ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    ScreenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    ScreenGui.Parent = playerGui

    ScreenGui.Destroying:Connect(function()
        autoSaveAlive = false
        saveConfig()
        ExecutorFixer.stop()
    end)

    local Main = Instance.new("Frame")
    Main.Name = "Main"
    Main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 215)
    Main.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 0.5, -107)
    Main.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 18)
    Main.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
    Main.BorderSizePixel = 0
    Main.Active = true
    Main.Draggable = true
    Main.ClipsDescendants = true
    Main.Parent = ScreenGui
    Instance.new("UICorner", Main).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)

    local MainStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    MainStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 90)
    MainStroke.Thickness = 1
    MainStroke.Transparency = 0.3
    MainStroke.Parent = Main

    local Background = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    Background.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    Background.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Background.Image = "rbxassetid://93857225029776"
    Background.ImageTransparency = 0.15
    Background.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    Background.ZIndex = 1
    Background.Parent = Main
    Instance.new("UICorner", Background).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)

    local Title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    Title.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 20)
    Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8)
    Title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Title.Text = "Sources Hub"
    Title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    Title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    Title.TextSize = 16
    Title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    Title.ZIndex = 10
    Title.Parent = Main

    local TitleStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    TitleStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    TitleStroke.Thickness = 1
    TitleStroke.Transparency = 0.5
    TitleStroke.Parent = Title

    local DiscordHolder = Instance.new("TextButton")
    DiscordHolder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 16)
    DiscordHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 28)
    DiscordHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    DiscordHolder.Text = "discord.gg/nxt1"
    DiscordHolder.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 150, 255)
    DiscordHolder.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    DiscordHolder.TextSize = 11
    DiscordHolder.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    DiscordHolder.AutoButtonColor = false
    DiscordHolder.ZIndex = 10
    DiscordHolder.Parent = Main

    local DcTextStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    DcTextStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    DcTextStroke.Thickness = 1
    DcTextStroke.Transparency = 0.5
    DcTextStroke.Parent = DiscordHolder

    local DiscordGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    DiscordGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(88, 101, 242)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(180, 200, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(88, 101, 242))
    })
    DiscordGrad.Parent = DiscordHolder

    task.spawn(function()
        local t = 0
        while DiscordHolder and DiscordHolder.Parent do
            t = t + 0.04
            DiscordGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t) * 0.6, 0)
            task.wait(0.03)
        end
    end)

    DiscordHolder.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if setclipboard then pcall(setclipboard, CONFIG.DISCORD)
        elseif toclipboard then pcall(toclipboard, CONFIG.DISCORD) end
        local old = DiscordHolder.Text
        DiscordHolder.Text = "Link copied!"
        task.delay(1.2, function() if DiscordHolder then DiscordHolder.Text = old end end)
    end)

    local CloseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    CloseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
    CloseBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 8)
    CloseBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    CloseBtn.Text = "X"
    CloseBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    CloseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    CloseBtn.TextSize = 13
    CloseBtn.ZIndex = 15
    CloseBtn.Parent = Main

    CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        stopExecution()
        ExecutorFixer.stop()
        autoSaveAlive = false
        saveConfig()
        ScreenGui:Destroy()
    end)

    local MinimizeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    MinimizeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
    MinimizeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0, 8)
    MinimizeBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    local function makePill(yPos, parent)
        local pill = Instance.new("Frame")
        pill.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 32)
        pill.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, yPos)
        pill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14)
        pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
        pill.ZIndex = 10
        pill.Parent = parent
        Instance.new("UICorner", pill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 70)
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Transparency = 0.5
        stroke.Parent = pill
        return pill
    end

    local function makeToggle(parent)
        local tpill = Instance.new("TextButton")
        tpill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 16)
        tpill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -44, 0.5, -8)
        tpill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
        tpill.Text = ""
        tpill.AutoButtonColor = false
        tpill.ZIndex = 11
        tpill.Parent = parent
        Instance.new("UICorner", tpill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
        dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12)
        dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -6)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
        dot.ZIndex = 12
        dot.Parent = tpill
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        return tpill, dot
    end

    local function makeLabel(parent, text, xOffset, width)
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, width or 160, 1, 0)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, xOffset or 12, 0, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = text
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.TextSize = 11
        lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        lbl.ZIndex = 11
        lbl.Parent = parent
        return lbl
    end

    local V1StatsPill = makePill(110, V1Container)
    local V1StatsLabel = makeLabel(V1StatsPill, "FPS: --   Ping: -- ms   TPB: 0", 12, 240)
    V1StatsLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    V1StatsLabel.TextSize = 9

    task.spawn(function()
        local frameCount = 0
        local lastT = tick()
        local conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            frameCount = frameCount + 1
        end)

        while true do
            task.wait(0.5)
            if not (V1StatsLabel and V1StatsLabel.Parent) then conn:Disconnect() break end
            local now = tick()
            local fps = math.floor(frameCount / (now - lastT))
            frameCount = 0
            lastT = now

            if not State.executorFixer then
                local p = measurePing()
                PingStats.current = p
            end
            local ping = math.floor(PingStats.current)
            local statsText = string.format("FPS: %d   Ping: %d ms   TPB: %d", fps, ping, PingStats.tpbCount)
            V1StatsLabel.Text = statsText
        end
    end)

    local function updatePower()
        PowerDisplay.Text = tostring(State.power[State.mode])
        V1PowerDisplay.Text = tostring(State.power[State.mode])
    end

    local function setMode(mode)
        State.mode = mode
        TweenService:Create(PcBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
            BackgroundColor3 = mode == "PC" and Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 85) or Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30),
            TextColor3 = mode == "PC" and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(MobileBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {
            BackgroundColor3 = mode == "Mobile" and Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 85) or Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30),
            TextColor3 = mode == "Mobile" and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
        }):Play()
        updatePower()
    end

    MinusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local maxP = State.mode == "PC" and CONFIG.MAX_POWER or 100000
        State.power[State.mode] = math.clamp(State.power[State.mode] - CONFIG.STEP, CONFIG.MIN_POWER, maxP)
        updatePower()
        if State.running and State.activeVersion == "V2" then stopExecution() startExecution() end
    end)

    PlusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local maxP = State.mode == "PC" and CONFIG.MAX_POWER or 100000
        State.power[State.mode] = math.clamp(State.power[State.mode] + CONFIG.STEP, CONFIG.MIN_POWER, maxP)
        updatePower()
        if State.running and State.activeVersion == "V2" then stopExecution() startExecution() end
    end)

    V1MinusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local maxP = State.mode == "PC" and CONFIG.MAX_POWER or 100000
        State.power[State.mode] = math.clamp(State.power[State.mode] - CONFIG.STEP, CONFIG.MIN_POWER, maxP)
        updatePower()
        if State.running and State.activeVersion == "V1" then stopExecution() startExecution() end
    end)

    V1PlusBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local maxP = State.mode == "PC" and CONFIG.MAX_POWER or 100000
        State.power[State.mode] = math.clamp(State.power[State.mode] + CONFIG.STEP, CONFIG.MIN_POWER, maxP)
        updatePower()
        if State.running and State.activeVersion == "V1" then stopExecution() startExecution() end
    end)

    V1PowerDisplay.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local num = tonumber(V1PowerDisplay.Text)
        if num then
            State.power[State.mode] = math.clamp(math.floor(num), CONFIG.MIN_POWER, CONFIG.MAX_POWER)
        end
        updatePower()
        if State.running and State.activeVersion == "V1" then stopExecution() startExecution() end
    end)

    V1DelayMinus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.v1Delay = math.clamp(State.v1Delay - 0.01, CONFIG.MIN_DELAY, CONFIG.MAX_DELAY)
        V1DelayDisplay.Text = string.format("%.3f", State.v1Delay)
        if State.running and State.activeVersion == "V1" then stopExecution() startExecution() end
    end)

    V1DelayPlus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.v1Delay = math.clamp(State.v1Delay + 0.01, CONFIG.MIN_DELAY, CONFIG.MAX_DELAY)
        V1DelayDisplay.Text = string.format("%.3f", State.v1Delay)
        if State.running and State.activeVersion == "V1" then stopExecution() startExecution() end
    end)

    V1DelayDisplay.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local num = tonumber(V1DelayDisplay.Text)
        if num then
            State.v1Delay = math.clamp(num, CONFIG.MIN_DELAY, CONFIG.MAX_DELAY)
            V1DelayDisplay.Text = string.format("%.3f", State.v1Delay)
        else
            V1DelayDisplay.Text = string.format("%.3f", State.v1Delay)
        end
        if State.running and State.activeVersion == "V1" then stopExecution() startExecution() end
    end)

    PcBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setMode("PC") end)
    MobileBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setMode("Mobile") end)

    local function setBypass(on)
        State.bypassEnabled = on
        State.running = on
        if on then
            BypassLabel.Text = "Bypass: ON"
            V1BypassLabel.Text = "Bypass: ON"

            TweenService:Create(ToggleDot, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, -6),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 130)
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(TogglePill, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 80, 50)}):Play()

            TweenService:Create(V1ToggleDot, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, -6),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 130)
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(V1TogglePill, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 80, 50)}):Play()
            
            startExecution()
        else
            BypassLabel.Text = "Bypass: OFF"
            V1BypassLabel.Text = "Bypass: OFF"

            TweenService:Create(ToggleDot, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -6),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(TogglePill, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)}):Play()

            TweenService:Create(V1ToggleDot, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -6),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(V1TogglePill, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)}):Play()
            
            stopExecution()
        end
    end

    TogglePill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setBypass(not State.bypassEnabled) end)
    V1TogglePill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setBypass(not State.bypassEnabled) end)

    local execGradAnim = nil

    local function setExecutorFixer(on)
        if on then
            ExecLabel.Text = "Executor Fixer: ON"
            TweenService:Create(ExecToggleDot, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, -6),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 80, 255)
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(ExecTogglePill, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 20, 90)
            }):Play()
            ExecGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.6)
            if execGradAnim then task.cancel(execGradAnim) end
            execGradAnim = task.spawn(function()
                local t = 0
                while State.executorFixer do
                    t = t + 0.05
                    ExecGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t) * 0.8, 0)
                    task.wait(0.03)
                end
            end)
            ExecutorFixer.start()
        else
            ExecLabel.Text = "Executor Fixer: OFF"
            TweenService:Create(ExecToggleDot, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -6),
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
            }):Play()
            TweenService:Create(ExecTogglePill, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
            }):Play()
            ExecGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1)
            if execGradAnim then pcall(task.cancel, execGradAnim) execGradAnim = nil end
            ExecutorFixer.stop()
        end
    end

    ExecTogglePill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        setExecutorFixer(not State.executorFixer)
    end)

    V1Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.activeVersion = "V1"
        V1Container.Visible = isOpen
        V2Container.Visible = false
        V1Btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 85)
        V1Btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        V2Btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
        V2Btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
    end)

    V2Btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.activeVersion = "V2"
        V1Container.Visible = false
        V2Container.Visible = isOpen
        V2Btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 85)
        V2Btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        V1Btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 30)
        V1Btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
    end)

    local listeningKeybind = false
    local listeningV1Keybind = false

    KeybindBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        listeningKeybind = true
        KeybindBtn.Text = "[...]"
        KeybindBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 80)
    end)

    V1KeybindBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        listeningV1Keybind = true
        V1KeybindBtn.Text = "[...]"
        V1KeybindBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 80)
    end)

    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
        if gp then return end
        if listeningKeybind and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            State.keybind = input.KeyCode
            KeybindBtn.Text = "[" .. input.KeyCode.Name .. "]"
            KeybindBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
            V1KeybindBtn.Text = "[" .. input.KeyCode.Name .. "]"
            listeningKeybind = false
            return
        end
        if listeningV1Keybind and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
            State.keybind = input.KeyCode
            V1KeybindBtn.Text = "[" .. input.KeyCode.Name .. "]"
            V1KeybindBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
            KeybindBtn.Text = "[" .. input.KeyCode.Name .. "]"
            listeningV1Keybind = false
            return
        end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode == State.keybind then
            setBypass(not State.bypassEnabled)
        end
    end)

    setMode(State.mode)
    setBypass(false)
    end)
    if not _pingOk then
        warn("[NXT] PING LEEGR failed to open: " .. tostring(_pingErr))
    end
end

-- AUTO PLY loader: keeps Auto Play (Auto Left/Right) and disables Auto Grab.
local AUTO_PLY_URL = "https://files.manuscdn.com/user_upload_by_module/session_file/310519663739806041/ZUjzHQaghZtIefyW.txt"
local autoPlyLoaded = false
local function runAutoPly()
    if autoPlyLoaded then
        local pg = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        local existing = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("NXT_AutoPlay")
        if existing then existing.Enabled = true end
        return true
    end
    local source = nxtHttpGet(AUTO_PLY_URL)
    if type(source) ~= "string" or #source < 100 then
        warn("[NXT] AUTO PLY failed to download source")
        return false
    end
    -- Disable the duplicate Auto Grab, keep Auto Play speed settings, and use the NXT1 link.
    source = source:gsub('local grabEnabled = savedToggle == nil and true or savedToggle', 'local grabEnabled = false')
    source = source:gsub('local DISCORD_LINK = "[^"]*"', 'local DISCORD_LINK = "https://discord.gg/nxt1"')
    source = source:gsub('DISCORD_LINK = "[^"]*"', 'DISCORD_LINK = "https://discord.gg/nxt1"')
    source = source:gsub('loadstring%(game:HttpGet%("https://voidexternal%.xyz/v2/scrap/24e5841c3e6c12d76540407f/raw"%)%)%(%)', '')
    local compiler = loadstring or load
    if type(compiler) ~= "function" then return false end
    local ok, chunk = pcall(compiler, source)
    if not ok or type(chunk) ~= "function" then return false end
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(chunk)
        task.delay(0.25, function()
            local pg = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            local function removeAutoGrabGui(container)
                if not container then return end
                local grabGui = container:FindFirstChild("NXT_Gui")
                if grabGui then grabGui:Destroy() end
            end
            removeAutoGrabGui(pg)
            pcall(function() removeAutoGrabGui(game:GetService("CoreGui")) end)
            pcall(function() if _gethui then removeAutoGrabGui(_gethui()) end end)
        end)
    end)
    autoPlyLoaded = true
    return true
end

    end

    local masterRow, masterTrack, masterBall, masterBtn = createToggleRow(1, "MASTER ENABLE", false)
    local antiBatRow, antiBatTrack, antiBatBall, antiBatBtn = createToggleRow(2, "Anti-Bat Shield", true)

    -- Additional row requested by the user; currently visual-only until its action is supplied.
    local pingLeegrRow, pingLeegrTrack, pingLeegrBall, pingLeegrBtn = createToggleRow(3, "PING LEEGR", false)
    pingLeegrBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        task.spawn(runPingLeegr)
    end)

    local rangeRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    rangeRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 17, 23)
    rangeRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    rangeRow.LayoutOrder = 4
    rangeRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
    rangeRow.ZIndex = 12
    rangeRow.Parent = containerFrame
    Instance.new("UICorner", rangeRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    local rangeStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    rangeStroke.Color = GREEN
    rangeStroke.Thickness = 1
    rangeStroke.Transparency = 0.55

    rangeLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    rangeLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 1, 0)
    rangeLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold

    rangeLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 247, 255)
    rangeLabel.TextSize = 12
    rangeLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    rangeLabel.ZIndex = 13
    rangeLabel.Parent = rangeRow

    local rangeBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    rangeBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 28, 36)
    rangeBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -80, 0.5, -12)
    rangeBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 24)
    rangeBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    rangeBox.Text = "28"
    rangeBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 247, 255)
    rangeBox.TextSize = 13
    rangeBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    rangeBox.ZIndex = 13
    rangeBox.Parent = rangeRow
    Instance.new("UICorner", rangeBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
    local boxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    boxStroke.Color = GREEN
    boxStroke.Thickness = 1
    boxStroke.Transparency = 0.4
    boxStroke.Parent = rangeBox

    local evasionRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    evasionRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 17, 23)
    evasionRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    evasionRow.LayoutOrder = 5
    evasionRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36)
    evasionRow.ZIndex = 12
    evasionRow.Parent = containerFrame
    Instance.new("UICorner", evasionRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    local evasionStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    evasionStroke.Color = GREEN
    evasionStroke.Thickness = 1
    evasionStroke.Transparency = 0.55
    evasionStroke.Parent = evasionRow

    local evasionLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    evasionLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    evasionLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
    evasionLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 1, 0)
    evasionLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    evasionLabel.Text = "Evasion Mode"
    evasionLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 247, 255)
    evasionLabel.TextSize = 12
    evasionLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    evasionLabel.ZIndex = 13
    evasionLabel.Parent = evasionRow

    local orbitalBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    orbitalBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 34, 44)
    orbitalBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -112, 0.5, -12)
    orbitalBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 24)
    orbitalBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    orbitalBtn.Text = "ORBITAL"
    orbitalBtn.TextColor3 = GREEN
    orbitalBtn.TextSize = 11
    orbitalBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
    orbitalBtn.ZIndex = 13
    orbitalBtn.Parent = evasionRow
    Instance.new("UICorner", orbitalBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)

    local freezeRow, freezeTrack, freezeBall, freezeBtn = createToggleRow(6, "Freeze Others", false)
    local flingRow, flingTrack, flingBall, flingBtn = createToggleRow(7, "Fling Engine", false)
    local antiDieRow, antiDieTrack, antiDieBall, antiDieBtn = createToggleRow(8, "Anti-Die", false)

    local statusRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    statusRow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 17, 23)
    statusRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    statusRow.LayoutOrder = 9
    statusRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
    statusRow.ZIndex = 12
    statusRow.Parent = containerFrame
    Instance.new("UICorner", statusRow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 9)

    local statusLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    statusLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    statusLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    statusLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    statusLabel.Text = "STATUS: OFFLINE"
    statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(145, 152, 166)
    statusLabel.TextSize = 11
    statusLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    statusLabel.ZIndex = 13
    statusLabel.Parent = statusRow

    local function setToggleVisual(track, ball, isOn)
        TweenService:Create(track, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
            BackgroundColor3 = isOn and Color3.fromRGB(24, 105, 52) or Color3.fromRGB(35, 39, 50)
        }):Play()
        TweenService:Create(ball, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Position = isOn and UDim2.new(0, 27, 0, 3) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3),
            BackgroundColor3 = isOn and GREEN or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        }):Play()
    end

    local function updateStatus()
        if State.masterEnabled then
            statusLabel.Text = "STATUS: ONLINE"
            statusLabel.TextColor3 = GREEN
        else
            statusLabel.Text = "STATUS: OFFLINE"
            statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(145, 152, 166)
        end
    end

    local function clearAntiDieConnections()
        if antiDieHealthConn then antiDieHealthConn:Disconnect() antiDieHealthConn = nil end
        if antiDieDiedConn then antiDieDiedConn:Disconnect() antiDieDiedConn = nil end
        if antiDieHeartbeatConn then antiDieHeartbeatConn:Disconnect() antiDieHeartbeatConn = nil end
    end

    local function bindResetButton(enabled)
        pcall(function()
            if enabled then
                StarterGui:SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", function() end)
            else
                StarterGui:SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", true)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function protectCharacter(character)
        clearAntiDieConnections()
        if not (State.antiDieEnabled and State.masterEnabled) then return end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid then return end
        bindResetButton(true)
        humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
        antiDieHealthConn = humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(health)
            if State.antiDieEnabled and State.masterEnabled and health < humanoid.MaxHealth then
                humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
            end
        end)
        antiDieDiedConn = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            if State.antiDieEnabled and State.masterEnabled then
                task.defer(function()
                    if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth end
                end)
            end
        end)
        antiDieHeartbeatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not (State.antiDieEnabled and State.masterEnabled) then return end
            if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
                if humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth then humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth end
                if humanoid:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                    humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function setAntiDie(state)
        State.antiDieEnabled = state
        if state and State.masterEnabled then
            bindResetButton(true)
            if LocalPlayer.Character then protectCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character) end
        else
            clearAntiDieConnections()
            bindResetButton(false)
        end
    end

    local function startAntiBat()
        if antiBatConn then antiBatConn:Disconnect() end
        lastSafeCFrame, lastCheckTime = nil, 0
        antiBatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not (State.antiBatActive and State.masterEnabled) then return end
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then return end
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
            local range = State.rangeValue or 28
            local now = tick()
            if hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude < 70 then
                lastSafeCFrame = hrp.CFrame
                lastCheckTime = now
            elseif hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude > 110 and lastSafeCFrame and (now - lastCheckTime) < 1.5 then
                hrp.CFrame = lastSafeCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.1, 0)
            end
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                    local eHrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local tool = plr.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
                    if eHrp and tool and tool.Name:lower():find("bat") then
                        if (hrp.Position - eHrp.Position).Magnitude < range then
                            if State.evasionMode == "ORBITAL" then
                                local angle = math.rad(tick() * 500)
                                hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(math.sin(angle) * 3, 0, math.cos(angle) * 3)
                            else
                                local offset = (hrp.Position - eHrp.Position).Unit * (range + 5)
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(eHrp.Position + offset + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function stopAntiBat()
        if antiBatConn then antiBatConn:Disconnect() antiBatConn = nil end
    end

    local function startFreeze()
        if freezeConn then freezeConn:Disconnect() end
        freezeConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not (State.freezeOthers and State.masterEnabled) then return end
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                    local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function stopFreeze()
        if freezeConn then freezeConn:Disconnect() freezeConn = nil end
    end

    local function startFling()
        if flingConn then flingConn:Disconnect() end
        flingConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not (State.flingEngine and State.masterEnabled) then return end
            local myHrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myHrp then return end
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                    local eHrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if eHrp and (myHrp.Position - eHrp.Position).Magnitude < (State.rangeValue or 28) then
                        eHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = (eHrp.Position - myHrp.Position).Unit * 150 + Vector3.new(0, 80, 0)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function stopFling()
        if flingConn then flingConn:Disconnect() flingConn = nil end
    end

    masterBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.masterEnabled = not State.masterEnabled
        setToggleVisual(masterTrack, masterBall, State.masterEnabled)
        updateStatus()
        if State.masterEnabled then
            if State.antiBatActive then startAntiBat() end
            if State.antiDieEnabled then setAntiDie(true) end
            if State.freezeOthers then startFreeze() end
            if State.flingEngine then startFling() end
        else
            stopAntiBat()
            setAntiDie(false)
            stopFreeze()
            stopFling()
        end
    end)

    antiBatBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.antiBatActive = not State.antiBatActive
        setToggleVisual(antiBatTrack, antiBatBall, State.antiBatActive)
        if State.antiBatActive and State.masterEnabled then startAntiBat() else stopAntiBat() end
    end)

    rangeBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        State.rangeValue = math.clamp(tonumber(rangeBox.Text) or 28, 1, 500)
        rangeBox.Text = tostring(State.rangeValue)
    end)

    orbitalBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.evasionMode = (State.evasionMode == "ORBITAL") and "TELEPORT" or "ORBITAL"
        orbitalBtn.Text = State.evasionMode
    end)

    freezeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.freezeOthers = not State.freezeOthers
        setToggleVisual(freezeTrack, freezeBall, State.freezeOthers)
        if State.freezeOthers and State.masterEnabled then startFreeze() else stopFreeze() end
    end)

    flingBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.flingEngine = not State.flingEngine
        setToggleVisual(flingTrack, flingBall, State.flingEngine)
        if State.flingEngine and State.masterEnabled then startFling() else stopFling() end
    end)

    antiDieBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local newState = not State.antiDieEnabled
        setToggleVisual(antiDieTrack, antiDieBall, newState)
        setAntiDie(newState)
    end)

    minimizeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        State.isMinimized = not State.isMinimized
        minimizeBtn.Text = State.isMinimized and "+" or "–"
        TweenService:Create(mainFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, State.isMinimized and MINI_HEIGHT or FULL_HEIGHT)
        }):Play()
    end)

    local dragInfo = {active = false}
    mainFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragInfo.active = true
            dragInfo.start = input.Position
            dragInfo.startPos = mainFrame.Position
        end
    end)
    mainFrame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragInfo.input = input
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if input == dragInfo.input and dragInfo.active then
            local delta = input.Position - dragInfo.start
            mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(dragInfo.startPos.X.Scale, dragInfo.startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, dragInfo.startPos.Y.Scale, dragInfo.startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragInfo.active = false
        end
    end)

    LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        task.wait(0.5)
        if State.antiDieEnabled and State.masterEnabled then protectCharacter(character) end
        if State.antiBatActive and State.masterEnabled then startAntiBat() end
    end)

    if LocalPlayer.Character and State.antiDieEnabled then protectCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character) end

    updateStatus()
    end)
    if not _nxtOk then
        warn("[NXT] Replacement submenu failed to open: " .. tostring(_nxtErr))
    end
end

local function nxtGetCustomAsset(path)
    if not path then return nil end
    local asset = nil
    pcall(function()
        if _getcustomasset then asset = _getcustomasset(path) end
    end)
    if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
    pcall(function()
        if syn and syn.getcustomasset then asset = syn.getcustomasset(path) end
    end)
    if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
    pcall(function()
        if getcustomasset then asset = getcustomasset(path, true) end
    end)
    if asset and asset ~= "" then return asset end
    return nil
end

-- Rebrand any small TP BAT interface left by an earlier injection.
pcall(function()
    local newName="NXTTpBat"
    for _,oldName in ipairs({"NXTTPBatV2GUI","CZBatTPGUI","EnvyAutoBatDesyncGUI"}) do
        for _,parent in ipairs({game:GetService("CoreGui"),LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")}) do
            local old=parent and parent:FindFirstChild(oldName)
            if old then
                old.Name=newName
                for _,obj in ipairs(old:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") then
                        if obj.Text=="CZ BAT TP" or obj.Text=="TP BAT V2" or obj.Text=="[CZ BAT TP]" then obj.Text="NXT TP BAT" end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

-- getconnections polyfill
getconnections = getconnections or get_signal_cons or getconnects
    or (syn and syn.get_signal_cons) or (getgenv and getgenv().getconnections)

local NXT_MENU_SONGS={
    {name="NXT Entry Audio",url="https://files.manuscdn.com/user_upload_by_module/session_file/310519663943627270/EipkEvXcMyfSQngR.mp3",file="nxt_menu_song_1.mp3"},
    {name="Screen Recording Audio",url="https://files.manuscdn.com/user_upload_by_module/session_file/310519663739806041/FIJGWFhtJKXVaUhx.mp3",file="nxt_screen_recording_audio.mp3"},
}
local nxtMenuSound
local nxtMenuMutedSounds={}
local nxtMenuMuteConn
local nxtMenuMuteStep
local function nxtMenuStopAudio()
    if nxtMenuSound then pcall(function() nxtMenuSound:Stop() end) end
    if nxtMenuMuteConn then nxtMenuMuteConn:Disconnect();nxtMenuMuteConn=nil end
    if nxtMenuMuteStep then nxtMenuMuteStep:Disconnect();nxtMenuMuteStep=nil end
    for sound,volume in pairs(nxtMenuMutedSounds) do
        pcall(function() if sound and sound.Parent then sound.Volume=volume end end)
    end
    nxtMenuMutedSounds={}
    nxtMenuMusicEnabled=false
    for _,setVisual in pairs(nxtMenuToggleVisuals) do pcall(function() setVisual(false) end) end
end
local function nxtMenuPlayAudio(index)
    if index then nxtMenuMusicIndex=index end
    local song=NXT_MENU_SONGS[nxtMenuMusicIndex] or NXT_MENU_SONGS[1]
    local asset=nxtGetCustomAsset(song.file)
    if not asset then
        local body=nxtHttpGet(song.url)
        if body and #body>1000 then
            pcall(function() if writefile then writefile(song.file,body) end end)
            asset=nxtGetCustomAsset(song.file)
        end
    end
    if not asset then return false end
    for _,obj in ipairs(game:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj:IsA("Sound") and obj~=nxtMenuSound then
            nxtMenuMutedSounds[obj]=obj.Volume;obj.Volume=0
        end
    end
    nxtMenuMuteConn=game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if obj:IsA("Sound") and obj~=nxtMenuSound then nxtMenuMutedSounds[obj]=obj.Volume;obj.Volume=0 end
    end)
    nxtMenuMuteStep=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        for sound in pairs(nxtMenuMutedSounds) do pcall(function() if sound.Parent then sound.Volume=0 end end) end
    end)
    if not nxtMenuSound then
        nxtMenuSound=Instance.new("Sound")
        nxtMenuSound.Name="NXTMenuMusic"
        nxtMenuSound.Looped=false
        nxtMenuSound.Parent=game:GetService("SoundService")
        nxtMenuSound.Ended:Connect(function()
            if nxtMenuMusicLoopEnabled and nxtMenuMusicEnabled then
                -- auto replay
                pcall(function()
                    nxtMenuSound.TimePosition=0
                    nxtMenuSound:Play()
                end)
                return
            end
            nxtMenuMusicEnabled=false
            if nxtMenuMuteConn then nxtMenuMuteConn:Disconnect();nxtMenuMuteConn=nil end
            if nxtMenuMuteStep then nxtMenuMuteStep:Disconnect();nxtMenuMuteStep=nil end
            for sound,volume in pairs(nxtMenuMutedSounds) do pcall(function() if sound.Parent then sound.Volume=volume end end) end
            nxtMenuMutedSounds={}
            for _,setVisual in pairs(nxtMenuToggleVisuals) do pcall(function() setVisual(false) end) end
        end)
    end
    nxtMenuSound.SoundId=asset
    nxtMenuSound.Volume=1
    nxtMenuSound.Looped = nxtMenuMusicLoopEnabled == true
    nxtMenuMusicEnabled=true
    for key,setVisual in pairs(nxtMenuToggleVisuals) do pcall(function() setVisual(key==nxtMenuMusicIndex) end) end
    nxtMenuSound:Play()
    return true
end

local NXT_LOGO_URL = "https://files.manuscdn.com/user_upload_by_module/session_file/310519663943627270/IULPjCxYoIdnqjpq.JPG"

local _nxtLogoAsset = nil
local function getNXTLogoAsset()
    if _nxtLogoAsset and _nxtLogoAsset ~= "" then return _nxtLogoAsset end
    pcall(function()
        if isfile and isfile(NXT_LOGO_FILE) then
            _nxtLogoAsset = nxtGetCustomAsset(NXT_LOGO_FILE)
        end
    end)
    if _nxtLogoAsset and _nxtLogoAsset ~= "" then return _nxtLogoAsset end
    local body = nxtHttpGet(NXT_LOGO_URL)
    if body and body ~= "" then
        pcall(function() writefile(NXT_LOGO_FILE, body) end)
        _nxtLogoAsset = nxtGetCustomAsset(NXT_LOGO_FILE)
    end
    return _nxtLogoAsset
end

local function getMenuBackgroundAsset(url,fileName)
    local asset=nil
    pcall(function() if isfile and isfile(fileName) then asset=nxtGetCustomAsset(fileName) end end)
    if asset and asset~="" then return asset end
    local body=nxtHttpGet(url)
    if body and body~="" then
        pcall(function() writefile(fileName,body) end)
        asset=nxtGetCustomAsset(fileName)
    end
    return asset
end

local function addNXTLogoText(parent, zIndex, transparency)
    local logo = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    logo.Name = "NXTLogoText"
    logo.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    logo.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    logo.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.86, 0.42)
    logo.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    logo.Text = "NXT"
    logo.TextColor3 = RED_BRIGHT
    logo.TextTransparency = transparency or 0
    logo.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    logo.TextScaled = true
    logo.ZIndex = zIndex or 1
    logo.Parent = parent
    local constraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
    constraint.MinTextSize = 28
    constraint.MaxTextSize = 110
    constraint.Parent = logo
    local logoStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    logoStroke.Color = BLACK
    logoStroke.Thickness = 2.5
    logoStroke.Transparency = 0.08
    logoStroke.Parent = logo
    return logo
end



local function destroyOldGui(name)
    if not name then return end
    pcall(function()
        if _gethui then
            local h = _gethui(); local o = h and h:FindFirstChild(name); if o then o:Destroy() end
        end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui"); local o = cg:FindFirstChild(name); if o then o:Destroy() end
    end)
    pcall(function()
        local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
        if plr then
            local pg = plr:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
            if pg then local o = pg:FindFirstChild(name); if o then o:Destroy() end end
        end
    end)
end

local SkyOrder={"Off","Night","Aurora","Sunset","Galaxy","Cyber","Sakura","Pink Night","Blood Moon","Emerald Dawn","Volcanic","Arctic","Midnight Ocean","Vaporwave","Toxic","Solar Eclipse","Hellscape","Heaven","Storm","Sunrise","Deep Space","Lavender Dream","Inferno","Mint Sky"}
local function nxtSkyColor(rgb) return Color3.fromRGB(rgb[1],rgb[2],rgb[3]) end
local function NXTApplyCustomSky(mode)
    for _,child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do if child:GetAttribute(NXT_SKY_TAG) then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end end
    local terrain=workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if terrain then for _,child in ipairs(terrain:GetChildren()) do if child:GetAttribute(NXT_SKY_TAG) then pcall(function() child:Destroy() end) end end end
    local preset=NXT_SKY_PRESETS[mode]
    if not preset or preset.kind=="off" then Lighting.ClockTime=14;Lighting.Brightness=2;Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=Color3.fromRGB(127,127,127);Lighting.Ambient=Color3.fromRGB(127,127,127);Lighting.FogEnd=100000;Lighting.GlobalShadows=true;return end
    Lighting.FogStart=0;Lighting.FogEnd=100000;Lighting.FogColor=Color3.fromRGB(200,200,200);Lighting.ColorShift_Top=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0);Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0);Lighting.GlobalShadows=true
    Lighting.ClockTime=preset.clock or 14;Lighting.Brightness=preset.brightness or 2
    if preset.outAmb then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient=nxtSkyColor(preset.outAmb) end
    if preset.ambient then Lighting.Ambient=nxtSkyColor(preset.ambient) end
    if preset.sky then
        local skyInst=Instance.new("Sky");skyInst:SetAttribute(NXT_SKY_TAG,true)
        if preset.sky.stars then skyInst.StarCount=preset.sky.stars end
        if preset.sky.moon then skyInst.MoonAngularSize=preset.sky.moon end
        if preset.sky.sun then skyInst.SunAngularSize=preset.sky.sun end
        if preset.sky.moonTex then skyInst.MoonTextureId="rbxasset://sky/moon.jpg" end
        skyInst.Parent=Lighting
    end
    if preset.atm then
        local atm=Instance.new("Atmosphere");atm:SetAttribute(NXT_SKY_TAG,true)
        atm.Density=preset.atm.dens or 0.3;atm.Color=nxtSkyColor(preset.atm.color);atm.Decay=nxtSkyColor(preset.atm.decay);atm.Glare=preset.atm.glare or 1;atm.Haze=preset.atm.haze or 1;atm.Parent=Lighting
    end
    if preset.clouds and terrain then
        local clouds=Instance.new("Clouds");clouds:SetAttribute(NXT_SKY_TAG,true)
        clouds.Cover=preset.clouds.cover or 0.5;clouds.Density=preset.clouds.dens or 0.5;clouds.Color=nxtSkyColor(preset.clouds.color);clouds.Parent=terrain
    end
end

currentGuiTheme = "Obsidian"

animPack = "Adidas Sports"
animPackEnabled = true
headlessEnabled = false
korbloxEnabled = false

local HEADLESS_MESH_ID = "rbxassetid://1095708"
local KORBLOX_MESH_ID = "rbxassetid://101851696"
local KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID = "rbxassetid://101851254"
local DARK_GREY_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(64, 64, 64)

local function removeFace(head)
    local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
    if face then face:Destroy() end
end

function applyHeadlessToChar(char, enabled)
    if not char then return end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then return end
    if enabled then
        head.Transparency = 1
        head.CanCollide = false
        removeFace(head)
        for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.MeshId == HEADLESS_MESH_ID then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        mesh.MeshId = HEADLESS_MESH_ID
        mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, 0.001)
        mesh.Name = "HeadlessMesh"
        mesh.Parent = head
        head:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
            if head.Transparency ~= 1 then head.Transparency = 1 end
        end)
        head.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "face" and child:IsA("Decal") then child:Destroy() end
        end)
    else
        head.Transparency = 0
        head.CanCollide = true
        for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "HeadlessMesh" then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        removeFace(head)
    end
end

function applyKorbloxToChar(char, enabled)
    if not char then return end
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return end
    if enabled then
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") or child:IsA("CharacterMesh") then child:Destroy() end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                rightLeg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Color"):Connect(function()
                    if rightLeg.Color ~= DARK_GREY_COLOR then rightLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR end
                end)
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = KORBLOX_MESH_ID
                mesh.TextureId = KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = rightLeg
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 1
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 1 end
                if rightFoot then rightFoot.Transparency = 1 end
                local oldKorblox = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if oldKorblox then oldKorblox:Destroy() end
                local korbloxLeg = Instance.new("Part")
                korbloxLeg.Name = "KorbloxLeg"
                korbloxLeg.Size = Vector3.new(1, 2, 1)
                korbloxLeg.Anchored = false
                korbloxLeg.CanCollide = false
                korbloxLeg.Color = DARK_GREY_COLOR
                korbloxLeg.Parent = char
                local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
                mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
                mesh.MeshId = KORBLOX_MESH_ID
                mesh.TextureId = KORBLOX_TEXTURE_ID
                mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
                mesh.Name = "KorbloxMesh"
                mesh.Parent = korbloxLeg
                local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
                weld.Part0 = rightUpperLeg
                weld.Part1 = korbloxLeg
                weld.C0 = CFrame.new(0, -0.8, 0)
                weld.Name = "KorbloxWeld"
                weld.Parent = korbloxLeg
            end
        end
    else
        if humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
            local rightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
            if rightLeg then
                for _, child in ipairs(rightLeg:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and child.Name == "KorbloxMesh" then child:Destroy() end
                end
                rightLeg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            end
        elseif humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
            local rightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
            if rightUpperLeg then
                rightUpperLeg.Transparency = 0
                local rightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
                local rightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
                if rightLowerLeg then rightLowerLeg.Transparency = 0 end
                if rightFoot then rightFoot.Transparency = 0 end
                local korbloxLeg = char:FindFirstChild("KorbloxLeg")
                if korbloxLeg then korbloxLeg:Destroy() end
            end
        end
    end
end

function applyCharterToChar(char)
    if not char then return end
    applyHeadlessToChar(char, headlessEnabled)
    applyKorbloxToChar(char, korbloxEnabled)
end
function waitForAnimate(char)
    for _ = 1, 40 do
        local a = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if a and a:FindFirstChild("idle") and a:FindFirstChild("run") and a:FindFirstChild("walk") then
            return a
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
    end
    return nil
end

function setAnim(animObj, id)
    if animObj and id then
        animObj.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
    end
end

function stopAllTracks(hum)
    if not hum then return end
    for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
    end
end

function ensureAnim(folder, name)
    if not folder then return nil end
    local a = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
    if not a then
        a = Instance.new("Animation")
        a.Name = name
        a.Parent = folder
    end
    return a
end

function ensureIdleSlots(idleFolder, n)
    if not idleFolder then return end
    n = n or 2
    for i=1,n do
        ensureAnim(idleFolder, "Animation" .. i)
    end
end

function pickAnim(pack, ...)
    for i = 1, select("#", ...) do
        local k = select(i, ...)
        local v = pack[k]
        if v ~= nil then return v end
    end
    return nil
end

local applyingAnim = false
function applyAnimPack(packName)
    if applyingAnim then return false end
    applyingAnim = true

    local pack = PACKS[packName]
    if not pack then
        applyingAnim = false
        return false
    end

    local char = LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    local animate = waitForAnimate(char)
    if not animate then
        applyingAnim = false
        return false
    end

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    stopAllTracks(hum)

    local runObj   = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("run"),   "RunAnim")
    local walkObj  = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("walk"),  "WalkAnim")
    local jumpObj  = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("jump"),  "JumpAnim")
    local fallObj  = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("fall"),  "FallAnim")
    local climbObj = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("climb"), "ClimbAnim")
    local swimObj  = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("swim"),     "Swim")
    local swimIdleObj = ensureAnim(animate:FindFirstChild("swimidle"), "SwimIdle")
    local idleFolder = animate:FindFirstChild("idle")

    setAnim(walkObj,  pickAnim(pack, "WalkAnim", "Walk"))
    setAnim(runObj,   pickAnim(pack, "RunAnim", "Run"))
    setAnim(jumpObj,  pickAnim(pack, "JumpAnim", "Jump"))
    setAnim(fallObj,  pickAnim(pack, "FallAnim", "Fall"))
    setAnim(climbObj, pickAnim(pack, "ClimbAnim", "Climb"))
    setAnim(swimObj,      pickAnim(pack, "Swim"))
    setAnim(swimIdleObj,  pickAnim(pack, "SwimIdle") or pickAnim(pack, "Swim"))

    if idleFolder then
        local a1 = pickAnim(pack, "Animation1")
        local a2 = pickAnim(pack, "Animation2")
        if a1 or a2 then
            ensureIdleSlots(idleFolder, 2)
            local id1 = a1 or a2
            local id2 = a2 or a1 or id1
            setAnim(idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), id1)
            setAnim(idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation2"), id2)
        elseif pack.Idle and #pack.Idle > 0 then
            ensureIdleSlots(idleFolder, math.max(2, #pack.Idle))
            setAnim(idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), pack.Idle[1])
            setAnim(idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation2"), pack.Idle[2] or pack.Idle[1])
            for i = 3, #pack.Idle do
                local a = idleFolder:FindFirstChild("Animation" .. i)
                if a then setAnim(a, pack.Idle[i]) end
            end
        end
    end

    animate.Disabled = true
    task.wait(0.06)
    animate.Disabled = false

    if hum then
        pcall(function()
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed)
            task.wait(0.03)
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end)
    end

    animPack = packName
    applyingAnim = false
    return true
end
